# Agents · P · part 9

991 entries, plan-qa-reviewer to planner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **plan-qa-reviewer**: QA-perspective four-mode scope review for FDA artifacts (sub-flow at L3; discipline child at L4). Returns one of SCOPE_EXPANSION / SELECTIVE_EXPANSION / HOLD_SCOPE / SCOPE_REDUCTION + headline + mode-specific fields per `_shared/four-mode-framework.md`. ([Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow-architecture/agents/plan-qa-reviewer.md))
- **plan-quality-checker**: Pragmatic reviewer checking whether a planning doc is executable as written. Asks "can someone implement this without re-deriving missing detail?" — not "is the architecture right?". Read-only. Part of the /plan-review fan-out. ([Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes](https://github.com/Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-quality-checker.md))
- **plan-quality-gates**: This repository treats a plan as an executable quality contract, not as persuasive prose. The following ten safeguards exist because repeated architecture review can still miss a requirement when the requirement has no concrete artifact or failing test. ([heywowyou/Odysseus](https://github.com/heywowyou/Odysseus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/plan-quality-gates.md))
- **plan-questioner**: Sharpen a problem statement by probing for ambiguities, edge cases, and missing constraints before planning begins. ([samjonester/dotfiles](https://github.com/samjonester/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/personal/pi/agents/plan-questioner.md) · ★5)
- **plan-react**: synthesizer react plan — composed from the synthesizer personality x react domain x plan phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/plan-react.md))
- **plan-reactumg**: ReactUMG UI 开发规划专家。输入完整的 UI 需求，输出详细开发计划文档。在规划新的 ReactUMG UI 功能时使用。 ([15195999826/LomoMarketplace](https://github.com/15195999826/LomoMarketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/UE_ReactUMG/agents/PlanReactUMG.md) · ★1)
- **plan-readback-agent**: Cold reader for one internal-layer document — reads it with nothing else in hand and says back what it understood must be built, marking every gap it filled in on its own. Issues no verdict and asks no question (read-only). ([brabos-ai/code-addiction](https://github.com/brabos-ai/code-addiction/blob/HEAD/workbench/agents/plan-readback-agent.md) · ★11)
- **plan-reader**: Reads docs/project-plan.md and extracts only the target phase's scope (name, capabilities, out-of-scope, deliverables, affected subprojects) plus one-line summaries of the immediate neighbor phases. Returns a compact structured summary so the main thread never loads the full project-plan. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reader.md) · ★28 · 11 copies)
- **plan-reader**: Reads an orchestre plan with plan-lint --reader and returns its JSON exactly as printed, digest included. The plan reader that opens a run first runs progress.mjs run-start, then progress.mjs apply, a script that applies the user's rulings and commits them itself, then plan-lint. Also reads the scope of a task branch (plan-lint --scope). Used by the workflow orchestre:run-phase. ([Archibald0/circle-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Archibald0/circle-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestre/agents/plan-reader.md) · ★11)
- **plan-reader**: Read-only summarizer for a Terraform or OpenTofu plan.json, a rendered Kubernetes diff, or Ansible check-mode output. Returns counts by action, every delete and replace, every change to stateful, IAM, or network resources, and anything outside the stated scope. Never runs a write. ([iuliandita/idstack](https://github.com/iuliandita/idstack/blob/HEAD/harness/opencode/agents/plan-reader.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **plan-reader**: Reads docs/plans/&lt;FEATURE-ID&gt;-plan.md and returns a compact digest — Goal, Task list, Decision State, Validation status, and Traceability gaps — without loading the whole plan into the caller's context. Use before review/execute, or whenever a stage needs the plan's shape but not its full body. ([lucasAguiar11/agent-skills](https://github.com/lucasAguiar11/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow-kit/agents/plan-reader.md))
- **plan-readiness-reviewer**: Independent reviewer that decides whether a change plan is ready to implement, using the plan-readiness-review method, and writes the change's readiness.md with "- Status: APPROVED | NEEDS-REVISION | BLOCKED". It is not the plan's author and ignores any verdict the caller suggests. Use when a change's tasks are written and the plan needs its readiness gate, or when the user asks "is this plan ready?" for a change folder. ([chacal88/devkit](https://github.com/chacal88/devkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/plan-readiness-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reality-auditor**: Checks that a change plan's claims about the current code — the "current behavior", "context" or "what exists" statements in proposal.md/design.md — are still true today, citing file:line, and returns an ACCURATE/OUTDATED verdict. Read-only. Use before a plan review or before implementing an older plan — "is this plan still accurate?", "audit the current state", "has the code drifted since we planned?". ([chacal88/devkit](https://github.com/chacal88/devkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/plan-reality-auditor.md))
- **plan-reality-checker**: Verifies that a planning doc's claims about the codebase are actually true. For every "we'll extend X" / "this fits Y pattern" / "use existing Z", searches the repo to confirm or refute. Catches planner hallucinations before implementation. Read-only. Part of the /plan-review fan-out. ([Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes](https://github.com/Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reality-checker.md))

- **plan-reviewer**: Plan PR(実装前の計画・ADR・受け入れケース追加)をレビューし、要件と計画の抜けを検出する。実装計画のレビューを依頼されたときに使用。 ([ebichan88/swiss-stage](https://github.com/ebichan88/swiss-stage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Reviews a lesson plan against the learner's record and preferences for pedagogical problems — re-teaching what is demonstrated, skipping what is not, steps too large, checks that don't produce evidence, sessions that don't fit the time budget, roots that assume a skill, reuse that trusts a status. - **plan-reviewer**: Plan critic who adversarially audits the planner's PLAN.md and the architect's ADRs for gaps, contradictions, hidden risk, and lazy steps, emitting non-binding severity-tagged notes (a critic, not a judge). - **plan-reviewer**: You review an implementation plan against the issue it claims to satisfy, *before any code exists*. You are adversarial about scope and acceptance criteria, not about style. You never write code and never edit files. Plan syntax (`AC:` line, single-path `S&lt;n&gt;`/`T&lt;n&gt;` lines, file existence) is checked mechanically by guards; spend your effort on meaning. ([fediim-dev/better-auth-kmp](https://github.com/fediim-dev/better-auth-kmp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **plan-reviewer**: Adversarial plan reviewer. Stress-tests architecture plans before implementation begins. Use when a plan needs validation against the actual codebase. ([Felag-Engineering/gleipnir](https://github.com/Felag-Engineering/gleipnir/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Read-only adversarial review of a plan document. Emits READY/NOT READY verdict + grounded findings. Authorized to flag ungrounded plans per Rule 7. ([fzymgc-house/fzymgc-house-skills](https://github.com/fzymgc-house/fzymgc-house-skills/blob/HEAD/dev-flow/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: 계획서를 실행 전에 기획·코드·디자인 중 지정받은 관점에서 회의적으로 검토하는 읽기 전용 에이전트. 호출할 때 프롬프트에 계획서 절대경로, 관점 이름, 사용자 요청 원문, 작업 기준 코드베이스 절대경로와 HEAD(코드와 대조할 때), 도구 호출 상한을 준다. work-plan이 검수 조건(바꿀 파일 10개 초과, 되돌리기 비싼 작업, 연구 판정선)에 해당할 때 호출한다. 문서·코드를 고치지 않고 판정만 보고한다.
